Eighty-eight years after this flight, the study between the Turkish Air Airlines Association and the Air Force Command in 2000 began as Turkey's Pilot's Day on April 26th. The Turkish Air Force, one of the number of air forces in the world capable of raising its pilots and even friendly and allied pilots, meets the need with the pilot training system that NATO and the United States have implemented in Air Force standards. The Turkish Air Force continues its pilot training with two main jet bases, T-37 and T-38 aircraft in Yıkli İzmir, SF-260, T-37 and T-38. Turkey's Forestry Co-operatives include 3,000 cooperatives (OR-KOOP) and 300,000 forest villagers connected to these cooperatives. Co-operatives, the representative of the forest villager during the state-run process of the forests of Turkey, perform 70 per cent of forest activity. In our country, mountain and forest villages are generally built on bumpy and steep terrain, so forest villages are known to lack infrastructure and education and health. Because the ownership of forest and forest businesses belongs to the state, the forest villager is trying to make a living in the forest, the state has provided. In order for the forest villager's labor to be rewarded, the forest peasant must be provided by the state in order to survive where they were born. In dense forest areas, climate conditions and geographical conditions hold an important place in the local economy of forestry. The forest is the only source of livelihood for the local forest peasant. In this context, regulations and decisions made regarding the forests also directly affect the forestry co-ops, the only organisation of the forest villager. Law number 6009 was adopted in 2010, and restrictions were imposed on the rights of the forest villagers to benefit from the forests. This must be corrected immediately. Because of the wrong policies that have been implemented in recent years, forest production has reached a point where the prices of production, incentives and base units are low, and the increase in gas and diesel prices has increased beyond profit. As a result, from villages to cities began their migration. Today, in our country, the income level is the lowest mass forest villagers. They can't even afford to be under social security umbrellas as part of the BUG-KUR that he's known for lack of income. In order to eliminate this problem, legal arrangements must be made to ensure that the forest peasant can be taken under social security. The loss of the forest villager's sweat has affected the gains of the forest co-ops. Many forest co-ops have faced closure due to their inability to pay their taxes. Forest villagers and co-ops have suffered a massive loss of income due to the recent government policy, when forest production has transformed into a subcontractor system for reasons such as planting and service. The forest villager was practically a contractor's worker. The arrangements are supposed to be aimed at increasing the income of the forest peasant, but at the point that comes, the contractor and subcontractor are seen to be aimed at making more profits. The arrangements must be made to give up these practices so that the forest peasant can get what he deserves. Cutbacks are not enough to bring significant income to the forest villagers, but they need to be raised to higher levels. While Turkey's cadastro was being built, forest villages failed to get the necessary priority, bringing problems. The reason why these villages have been working in cadastro studies over the past few years is because of the legislation that these lands are often faced with the challenges of measuring land and identifying them in the name of the treasure. The problems mentioned briefly above are some of the problems of forest villagers and forestry cooperatives. In an effort to investigate the problems of forest villagers and forestry co-ops and to determine the solution proposals, the 98th Constitution. And 104 of Turkey's Parliamentary Council of the National Assembly. And 105. In accordance with the clauses, we offer and propose the opening of a parliamentary investigation. Dear lawmakers, as it is known, the penal code contains sanctions that interfere deeply with the individual's rights and freedoms. The choice of criminal law in the field of punishment, criminal judgment and criminal execution law is relevant to the state's criminal policy because of the culture and the level of civilization concerned with society's social and economic life. In other words, the rules regarding the execution of the law state's security measures and the rules of their execution are determined according to the criminal policy of the country's social, cultural structure, ethical values and the needs of economic life, particularly the principles of criminal law and the rules of the Constitution. According to this, legal regulations on the type and quantity of punishment and execution of acts that violate public order, trust and peace in society are fundamentally a matter of the state's crime and punishment policy. According to this, the legislator will determine acts that constitute crime according to the conditions of the day and the needs of society and will prevent crimes from being committed and will legalise punishments that will bring criminals to society. Criminal legislation should be based on a criminal and criminal policy aimed at protecting human rights and social security, highlighting the needs of the age and the common values of the reinvented nation and humanity. The main goal of punishment is to ensure an active regret for his crime and re-enact it, so the execution regime must be organised accordingly. Dear lawmakers, the new criminal legislation that took effect in 2005 has been redacted on the basis of this understanding. Modern approaches to punishment and rehabilitation policies have been adopted through the new criminal justice system established by the Turkish Penal Code and Penal Code. On the other hand, the Law on Execution of Penalty and Security Measures No. 5275 has been established as a legal infrastructure for a modern execution system with the Law on Disciplinary Freedom and Help Centres 4402 and the Protection Boards Act. In Article 5237, sanctions to be carried out in accordance with the Turkish Penal Code have been determined as punishment and security measures. In the Law on the execution of punishment and security measures, the procedures and foundations for the execution of punishment and security measures are also detailed. Dear lawmakers, as well as the effectiveness of the penal system in modern democracies, humanism is an important factor. In new approaches to the criminal execution system, the prevention of rehabilitation, re-enactment of criminals and re-criminalization is prerequisite. The legislation on the execution of punishment and measures consists of provisions that include the execution of punishment and administrative duties and activities, as well as the executions of criminal and material and measures, as a result of sentences carried out under the criminal justice system. The offer we're meeting today brings two arrangements. First, there has been a limitation on the relationship of prisoners to their lawyers or to those who have been given the right to meet with them according to the Penal Code. The second is that both prisoners and prisoners will be allowed in what circumstances, if they suffer from a severe illness, with the death or documentation of their relatives to a certain extent. A new joke is added as a sixth joke to Article 59 of the Law on the Execution of Penalty and Security Measures, Article 1 of the Proposition and 5275. This joke is estimated to limit the right to talk to a lawyer or others in the event of concrete facts that a criminal organization is guiding the activities of a criminal organization through people who are interviewing him, and the procedures and foundations of this limitation are regulated. It is also a guarantee that the authority to decide this limitation is given to a judge and that the way to appeal against these decisions is clear. If his conviction needs a lawyer at this time, a lawyer appointed by the Turkish Bar Association will be provided to benefit from legal aid. Article 93 of Law No. 5275, titled permissions, included regulations on how to use these permits, including warrants, special permits, or employment warrants for prisoners except those found at high-security execution institutions. In Article 116 of the Law, the obligations of the prisoners have been arranged and the provisions of the same Law can also be applied on the prisoners in the form of imprisonment of the provisions issued in 9, 16, 21, 26, and on various articles of the Law.
